The bracketed sequence [unleserlich] indicates that the original text contained an illegible or irrecoverable passage. The editor used "unleserlich" (German for "unreadable") to signal this omission. **Explanation:**  
The bracketed [sic] indicates the editor's intervention to signal an error in the original text. Since the original word "they" is present in the transcribed text and the [sic] is the editor's addition to highlight an apparent mistake, this falls under **Correction** (Rule 2). The original text's "they" is preserved, and the [sic] is encoded as a `<sic>` tag without a `<corr>` because the correction is not explicitly provided in the context.
